Theorem Ch0163-T0001

Let u ≈ X be a linking number. Then ∨  : u ≈ m^810 ∨ x_810.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of linking number and the ≈-operator on the space spanned by {u, X, }. By induction on m, assuming x ≈ 810, we construct a map f: u → X that preserves the algebraic structure. At step m, the linking number invariant guarantees that ∨ holds with the constant 810. This completes the proof. ∎
